• Step 4（右侧视频工作室与单并发队列监控）：实现当前问答的 MP4 视频播放器，以及下方的视频任务队列面板，严格按照 DESIGN.md 的成对功能色块与线性图标展示 queued（排队位次与前方人数）、running/processing（实时进度条）、done/completed（可播放）和失败状态。
• Step 5（Vitest 单元测试与构建检查）：编写覆盖队列状态流转展示、引用卡片展开、SSE 实时更新与历史切换的单元测试，运行 npm test 与 npm run build 确保零报错。

1	# 全局研发宪法（Global Engineering Constitution）
2	
3	> 本文件是本项目的最高工程法典。所有 Agent（含后续并行角色）与人在提交任何代码前，
4	> 必须已读并遵守本文件。违反铁律的代码不得合并。
5	
6	## 一、项目定位
7	
8	知识库问答与自动问答视频生成工作台：
9	
10	- 用户上传文档（`.md` / `.docx` / `.doc`），系统解析入库；
11	- 用户提问，系统基于知识库回答，**必须给出引用溯源（哪个文档、哪个段落）**；
12	- 每个问答自动生成一段简单的问答视频；
13	- 页面展示回答进度与视频生成进度，可查看历史记录；
14	- **全局单并发视频队列**：同一时刻只允许一个视频合成任务，先进先出排队。
15	
16	## 二、技术选型（锁定，不可擅自变更）
17	
18	| 层 | 选型 | 说明 |
19	| --- | --- | --- |
20	| 后端 | FastAPI + Python 3.11+ | 异步框架，SSE 原生友好 |
21	| 数据库 | SQLite（`backend/storage/app.db`） | 单文件、零运维；表结构见 `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` |
22	| 检索 | **零外部 Embedding 依赖**的本地混合检索 | BM25 + TF-IDF 稀疏向量本地实现；稠密向量可选但不强制 |
23	| 视频队列 | 单并发 + `asyncio.Lock()` | 全局一把锁，排队 FIFO |
24	| 前端 | React + TypeScript + Tailwind CSS | 编辑型工作台审美见 `DESIGN.md` / `design/tokens.css` |
25	| LLM | 通过 `LLM_BASE_URL` 调用（OpenAI 兼容协议） | Key 只经 `.env` 注入，禁止硬编码 |
26	
27	## 三、工程铁律（Hard Rules）
28	
29	1. **严禁 Mock 数据。** 任何测试、演示、截图都不得使用伪造的问答结果或伪造的视频文件
30	   冒充系统产出。测试数据必须走真实入库→检索→问答链路生成，或使用 `知识库/` 真实文档。
31	2. **全局单并发视频队列锁。** `video_tasks` 同一时刻 `running` 状态的任务数 ≤ 1；
32	   新任务必须进入 `queued` 排队，由调度器按 FIFO 提升。禁止绕过锁直接起第二条合成线程。
33	3. **核心业务逻辑单元测试覆盖率 ≥ 85%。** 覆盖对象至少包括：解析器（含注入隔离）、
34	   切块、混合检索、引用溯源、问答编排、视频队列调度。用 `pytest --cov` 度量，
35	   `backend/tests/` 为唯一测试目录。
36	4. **引用溯源强制。** 任何回答必须携带 `doc_id` + 原始文件名 + `heading_path` + `para_id`；
37	   无溯源的答案视为不合格。
38	5. **防注入强制。** 检索上下文与 System Prompt 的双重防御为架构级必选项（见
39	   `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §7），不得以"文档可信"为由省略。
40	
41	## 四、盲测隔离（Anti-Data-Leakage）—— 最高优先级
42	
43	为防止开发过程对测试集过拟合（"带着答案做题"），实施严格隔离：
44	
45	- **`docs/EVAL.md` 是盲测黄金评估集**（含 14 条用例与评分要点）。
46	- **前端开发 Agent、后端开发 Agent 严禁读取 `docs/EVAL.md`**，也不得读取任何从
47	  `docs/EVAL.md` 派生的文件（含答案、期望输出、评分脚本的期望断言）。
48	- 前端/后端 Agent 只能依据 `docs/API_SPEC.md`、`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`、`DESIGN.md`、
49	  `design/tokens.*` 与 `知识库/` 真实文档进行开发。
50	- **Eval 验收 Agent** 是唯一允许读取 `docs/EVAL.md` 的角色；其产出（验收报告）
51	  只允许写入 `evals/reports/`，且报告中不得反向泄露用例答案到开发 Agent 可读区域。
52	- 任何"为了通过某个用例而修改通用逻辑"的行为视为过拟合，违反即退回重做。
53

1	# 编辑型工作台设计规范（NotebookLM 参考系）
2	
3	本文件是项目唯一的设计依据。所有页面、幻灯片、图形与视频动效都从这里取值，
4	令牌定义在 `design/tokens.css` 与 `design/tokens.json`，三者必须保持一致。
5	
6	> 机器可读性：`scripts/gen_tokens.py` 从本文件 §3 色彩表、§4 字体层级表、
7	> §5 间距/圆角/描边/阴影、§7 动效正文提取令牌并生成两个实体文件；
8	> `scripts/check_tokens.py` 做三方 100% 一致性校验。改本文件后须重跑生成与校验。
9	
10	## 1. 来源与适用范围
11	
12	- 提取对象：一张 NotebookLM（Gemini Notebook）界面截图，三栏工作台布局。
13	- 提取方式：像素目测 + 版式测量，属近似值，见第 10 节可信度说明。
14	- 适用：信息密度高、以阅读与生成为主的产品界面、内部工具、研究报告页、说明型视频。
15	- 不适用：营销落地页、需要强视觉冲击的品牌站、电商详情页。
16	
17	## 2. 风格定位
18	
19	文档优先的编辑型工作台：白底、细线分区、克制的圆角、极低饱和的表面层次，
20	彩色只出现在功能图标与少量链接上。视觉信息全部由排版、留白与 1px 描边承担，
21	不靠阴影、渐变、插画或装饰图形。整体读起来像阅读器或专业工具，而不是营销页面。
22	
23	三条不可让渡的原则：
24	
25	1. 内容优先。任何装饰如果不能让信息更清楚，就不出现。
26	2. 层次靠线和留白，不靠阴影和色块堆叠。
27	3. 颜色是信号，不是背景。彩色面积不超过屏幕的 5%。
28	
29	## 3. 色彩
30	
31	| 角色 | 令牌 | 值 | 用途 |
32	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
33	| 页面底色 | `--nb-bg` | `#FFFFFF` | 主内容区、卡片 |
34	| 次级底 | `--nb-bg-subtle` | `#F7F8F9` | 侧栏、面板底、输入区 |
35	| 悬停填充 | `--nb-bg-hover` | `#F0F4F9` | 列表项 hover、选中行 |
36	| 描边 | `--nb-border` | `#E1E3E6` | 所有分区线、卡片边框 |
37	| 强描边 | `--nb-border-strong` | `#C9CCD1` | 输入框聚焦前、表格线 |
38	| 主文字 | `--nb-text` | `#1F1F1F` | 标题、正文主句 |
39	| 次文字 | `--nb-text-secondary` | `#444746` | 正文段落 |
40	| 说明文字 | `--nb-text-tertiary` | `#5E5E5E` | 元信息、时间、计数 |
41	| 强调色 | `--nb-accent` | `#1A73E8` | 链接、主按钮、焦点环 |
42	| 强调悬停 | `--nb-accent-hover` | `#1765CC` | 按钮与链接 hover |
43	| 强调浅底 | `--nb-accent-soft` | `#E8F0FE` | 选中态、标签底 |
44	
45	功能色块（用于来源类型、生成物条目的图标底）：底色浅、图标深一档，成对使用。
46	
47	| 名称 | 底 | 图标 |
48	| --- | --- | --- |
49	| 琥珀 | `--nb-tint-amber-bg` `#FEF0D3` | `--nb-tint-amber-ink` `#8A5A00` |
50	| 紫 | `--nb-tint-violet-bg` `#EDE7FB` | `--nb-tint-violet-ink` `#5B3E9B` |
51	| 绿 | `--nb-tint-green-bg` `#E4F3E8` | `--nb-tint-green-ink` `#1E6B3A` |
52	| 青 | `--nb-tint-teal-bg` `#DEF1F3` | `--nb-tint-teal-ink` `#0F5F68` |
53	| 蓝 | `--nb-tint-blue-bg` `#E3EEFC` | `--nb-tint-blue-ink` `#174EA6` |
54	
55	规则：正文与背景对比 ≥ 4.5:1，大字号与图标 ≥ 3:1；不得只用颜色区分状态，
56	必须同时有文字或图标；深色模式必须单独校验，不能由浅色模式推导。
57	
58	## 4. 字体与层级

```

1	# API 契约（API_SPEC）
2	
3	> 版本：v1.0（Session 1 基线，前后端完全对齐的唯一事实来源）
4	> 配套：`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`（数据模型与队列）、`CLAUDE.md`（工程宪法）
5	> 变更流程：任何字段/事件变更须先改本文件并评审，再改实现。
6	
7	## 1. 通用约定
8	
9	- Base URL：`http://localhost:8000`（后端 FastAPI）
10	- 无用户/认证模块（题目要求"直接提问"），所有接口免鉴权
11	- 请求/响应体均为 `application/json; charset=utf-8`
12	- 时间格式：RFC 3339 UTC（`2026-11-18T10:00:00Z`）
13	- 问答与视频进度走 **SSE**（`text/event-stream`），见 §6
14	
15	### 1.1 通用错误模型
16	```json
17	{ "error": { "code": "E_PARSE_FAILED", "message": "人类可读说明", "detail": {} } }
18	```
19	| code | 含义 |
20	| --- | --- |
21	| E_VALIDATION | 参数校验失败 |
22	| E_PARSE_FAILED | 文档解析失败（三级容错均失败） |
23	| E_LLM_FAILED | LLM 调用失败 |
24	| E_OUT_OF_KNOWLEDGE | 知识库未覆盖（安全拒答，非错误） |
25	| E_VIDEO_QUEUE_BUSY | 视频队列繁忙（已进入排队，非错误） |
26	| E_NOT_FOUND | 资源不存在 |
27	
28	## 2. 文档 API
29	
30	### POST /api/documents
31	上传文档入库。`Content-Type: multipart/form-data`，字段 `file`。
32	- 解析走三级容错（`.md` → `.docx` → `.doc` 转 docx），命中注入特征的段落被隔离不进检索语料，`documents.injection_flag=1`。
33	- 响应 `201`：
34	```json
35	{
36	  "document": {
37	    "doc_id": "doc08",
38	    "title": "西海市众赢科技有限公司介绍",
39	    "source_file": "西海市众赢科技有限公司介绍.docx",
40	    "source_format": "docx",
41	    "parse_method": "OOXML(word/document.xml) 解析",
42	    "char_count": 8766,
43	    "injection_flag": 1,
44	    "chunk_count": 74,
45	    "created_at": "2026-11-18T10:00:00Z"
46	  }
47	}
48	```
49	
50	### GET /api/documents
51	文档列表。响应 `200`：`{ "documents": [ <document> ], "total": 7 }`
52	
53	### GET /api/documents/{doc_id}
54	单文档详情。响应 `200`：`{ "document": <document>, "injection_excerpt_count": 1 }`
55	
56	### DELETE /api/documents/{doc_id}
57	删除文档及其 chunks、关联问答保留但引用标记失效。响应 `204`。
58	
59	## 3. 问答 API
60	
61	### POST /api/ask
62	提问。请求体：
63	```json
64	{ "question": "众赢科技 2025 年研发投入占营收比重是多少？", "history_limit": 10 }
65	```
66	响应：`text/event-stream`（SSE），事件序列见 §6。
67	
68	### GET /api/qa
69	历史问答列表。响应 `200`：
70	```json
71	{ "records": [ { "id": 1, "question": "…", "answer_summary": "…", "video_task_id": 3, "video_status": "done", 
```

1	# 知识库问答与自动问答视频生成工作台
2	
3	上传文档 → 基于知识库问答（带引用溯源）→ 自动生成问答视频；单并发视频队列、全局排队。
4	
5	## 入口
6	
7	| 文件 | 作用 |
8	| --- | --- |
9	| `CLAUDE.md` | 全局研发宪法 + 多 Agent 路由表（含盲测隔离禁区） |
10	| `DESIGN.md` | 编辑型工作台设计规范（设计令牌唯一事实来源） |
11	| `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` | 系统架构：4 张表 / 三级容错解析 / 混合检索 / 双重防注入 / 视频队列 |
12	| `docs/API_SPEC.md` | REST + SSE 前后端对齐契约 |
13	| `docs/EVAL.md` | ★盲测黄金评估集（14 条用例；前端/后端 Agent 禁读） |
14	| `design/tokens.css` / `tokens.json` | 设计令牌实体文件（由 DESIGN.md 生成，三方校验一致） |
15	| `docs/SESSION_LOG.md` | 全流程 AI 协作 Session 记录 |
16	
17	## 运行（基线验证）
18	
19	```bash
20	# 后端
21	cd backend && pip install -r requirements.txt
22	cp ../.env.example .env   # 填 LLM_API_KEY 等
23	python -m uvicorn app.main:app --port 8000     # GET /health
24	python -m pytest -q                            # 冒烟测试
25	
26	# 令牌三方一致性校验（任意目录）
27	python scripts/check_tokens.py
28	```
29	
30	## 工程铁律（摘要，详见 CLAUDE.md §三）
31	
32	严禁 Mock 数据 ｜ 全局单并发视频队列锁 ｜ 核心业务逻辑单测覆盖率 ≥ 85% ｜ 引用溯源强制 ｜ 防注入强制。
33	
34	## 状态

37	业务实现（解析/检索/问答/视频队列）按 `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` 与 `docs/API_SPEC.md` 在后续会话迭代。
38	
```

1	以下是一个客户发送的需求：
2	
3	我们需要一个知识库问答系统网页，可以上传文档。用户问一个问题，系统根据知识库来回答问题，系统不但要返回答案，还要说出引用参考了哪个文档。同时给问题到答案 生成一个简单的问答视频。
4	
5	不需要做用户等模块，直接提问即可，要看到回答进度和视频生成进度，可查看历史记录。
6	
7	网页不能太简陋，要有自己的审美，同时AI感不能太强。
8	
9	每次只允许生成一个视频，不允许并发视频任务，有新的视频任务进来应该做合理的排队处理。
10	
11	系统要能正常的回答问题，不要使用mock, 如果需要用到AI，代码自行附上一个临时的短期（24小时以内就行）AI key 如果不愿意赋key,也可提供一个短期临时的在线演示地址

1	# 系统架构设计（ARCHITECTURE）
2	
3	> 版本：v1.0（Session 1 基线）
4	> 配套文件：`docs/API_SPEC.md`（接口契约）、`CLAUDE.md`（工程宪法）、`DESIGN.md`（前端设计规范）
5	
6	## 1. 物理隔离的目录结构
7	
8	```
9	伏光-面试题/
10	├── CLAUDE.md                  # 全局研发宪法（多 Agent 路由表在此）
11	├── DESIGN.md                  # 编辑型工作台设计规范（色板/字体/间距/圆角源头）
12	├── design/
13	│   ├── tokens.css             # 设计令牌（CSS 自定义属性）
14	│   └── tokens.json            # 设计令牌（机器可读，供校验与构建消费）
15	├── docs/
16	│   ├── ARCHITECTURE.md        # 本文件
17	│   ├── API_SPEC.md            # REST + SSE 契约
18	│   └── EVAL.md                # ★盲测黄金评估集（前端/后端 Agent 禁读）
19	├── evals/                     # Eval 验收 Agent 专用（评估运行器/报告）
20	│   └── reports/
21	├── scripts/                   # 工具脚本（令牌校验、解析验证、评估运行器等）
22	├── backend/
23	│   ├── app/
24	│   │   ├── main.py            # FastAPI 入口
25	│   │   ├── config.py          # 配置（.env 读取）
26	│   │   ├── db.py              # SQLite 连接与建表
27	│   │   ├── models.py          # 4 张表的 ORM/行模型
28	│   │   ├── parsers/           # .md / .docx / .doc 三级容错解析
29	│   │   ├── ingestion.py       # 入库 + 注入隔离 + 切块
30	│   │   ├── retrieval.py       # 本地混合检索（BM25 + TF-IDF，零外部 Embedding）
31	│   │   ├── qa.py              # 问答编排（双重防注入 + JSON Schema 输出）
32	│   │   ├── video_queue.py     # 单并发视频合成队列（asyncio.Lock）
33	│   │   └── sse.py             # SSE 事件流
34	│   ├── tests/                 # ★唯一测试目录（覆盖率 ≥ 85%）
35	│   └── storage/
36	│       ├── uploads/           # 上传文档
37	│       ├── videos/            # 生成视频（不入库）
38	│       └── app.db             # SQLite（.gitignore 忽略）
39	└── frontend/
40	    └── src/                   # React + TS + Tailwind
41	```
42	
43	## 2. 数据模型（4 张 SQLite 表）
44	
45	### documents
46	| 字段 | 类型 | 说明 |
47	| --- | --- | --- |
48	| id | INTEGER PK | 内部主键 |
49	| doc_id | TEXT UNIQUE | 对外语料编号（如 doc01） |
50	| title | TEXT | 文档标题 |
51	| source_file | TEXT | 原始文件名（含扩展名） |
52	| source_format | TEXT | md / docx / doc |
53	| parse_method | TEXT | 解析方式说明 |
54	| char_count | INTEGER | 有效字数 |
55	| injection_flag | INTEGER | 是否含疑似越权指令文本（0/1
```

22	   D 文档内干扰抗性×2 / E 域外边界拒答×2 / F 视频排队压测×2），全部锚定 `知识库/` 真实文档。

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/lib/api.ts", "content": "/**\n * REST 客户端 —— 路径与字段严格对齐 docs/API_SPEC.md。\n * 仅做传输层，不做业务状态管理。\n */\nimport type { DocumentRecord, QaRecordDetail, QaRecordSummary, VideoTask } from \"../types\";\n\nconst BASE = \"/api\";\n\nexport class ApiError extends Error {\n  code: string;\n  detail: unknown;\n  constructor(code: string, message: string, detail?: unknown) {\n    super(message);\n    this.code = code;\n    this.detail = detail;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {\n  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, init);\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    let body: unknown = null;\n    try {\n      body = await res.json();\n    } catch {\n      /* 非 JSON 错误体 */\n    }\n    const err = (body as { error?: { code?: string; message?: string; detail?: unknown } })?.error;\n    throw new ApiError(err?.code ?? `E_HTTP_${res.status}`, err?.message ?? res.statusText, err?.detail);\n  }\n  if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;\n  return (await res.json()) as T;\n}\n\n/* ---------- 文档（§2） ---------- */\n\nexport async function uploadDocument(file: File): Promise<{ document: DocumentRecord }> {\n  const form = new FormData();\n  form.append(\"file\", file);\n  return request(\"/documents\", { method: \"POST\", body: form });\n}\n\nexport async function listDocuments(): Promise<{ documents: DocumentRecord[]; total: number }> {\n  return request(\"/documents\");\n}\n\nexport async function deleteDocument(docId: string): Promise<void> {\n  return request(`/documents/${encodeURIComponent(docId)}`, { method: \"DELETE\" });\n}\n\n/* ---------- 问答（§3） ---------- */\n\nexport async function listQaRecords(): Promise<{ records: QaRecordSummary[]; total: number }> {\n  return request(\"/qa\");\n}\n\nexport async function getQaRecord(id: number): Promise<{ record: QaRecordDetail }> {\n  return request(`/qa/${id}`);\n}\n\n/* ---------- 视频任务（§4） ---------- */\n\nexport async function listVideoTasks(): Promise<{ tasks: VideoTask[]; running_count: number }> {\n  return request(\"/video-tasks\");\n}\n\nexport async function getVideoTask(id: number): Promise<{ task: VideoTask }> {\n  return request(`/video-tasks/${id}`);\n}\n\nexport function videoFileUrl(taskId: number): string {\n  return `${BASE}/videos/${taskId}/file`;\n}\n"}

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/lib/sse.ts", "content": "/**\n * SSE 客户端 —— 事件名与负载字段 100% 对齐 docs/API_SPEC.md §6。\n * POST /api/ask 返回 text/event-stream：event: <type>\\ndata: <json>\\n\\n\n */\nimport type { SseEventName, SsePayloadMap } from \"../types\";\n\nexport type SseHandler<E extends SseEventName> = (payload: SsePayloadMap[E]) => void;\n\nexport interface SseSession {\n  /** 主动终止流（组件卸载 / 重复提问时调用） */\n  abort: () => void;\n}\n\n/**\n * 发起一次问答 SSE 流。onEvent 按事件名分发；onError 收到 `error` 事件负载；\n * 流关闭（done 或异常）后调用 onClose。\n */\nexport function askStream(\n  question: string,\n  historyLimit: number,\n  handlers: {\n    onEvent: <E extends SseEventName>(event: E, payload: SsePayloadMap[E]) => void;\n    onError?: (payload: SsePayloadMap[\"error\"]) => void;\n    onClose?: () => void;\n  },\n): SseSession {\n  const controller = new AbortController();\n\n  (async () => {\n    let res: Response;\n    try {\n      res = await fetch(\"/api/ask\", {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ question, history_limit: historyLimit }),\n        signal: controller.signal,\n      });\n    } catch (err) {\n      if ((err as Error).name !== \"AbortError\") handlers.onClose?.();\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!res.ok || !res.body) {\n      handlers.onError?.({ code: `E_HTTP_${res.status}`, message: res.statusText });\n      handlers.onClose?.();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const reader = res.body.getReader();\n    const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n    let buffer = \"\";\n\n    try {\n      for (;;) {\n        const { done, value } = await reader.read();\n        if (done) break;\n        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });\n        // SSE 以空行分隔事件块\n        let idx: number;\n        while ((idx = buffer.indexOf(\"\\n\\n\")) !== -1) {\n          const block = buffer.slice(0, idx);\n          buffer = buffer.slice(idx + 2);\n          dispatchBlock(block, handlers);\n        }\n      }\n    } finally {\n      reader.releaseLock();\n      handlers.onClose?.();\n    }\n  })();\n\n  return { abort: () => controller.abort() };\n}\n\nfunction dispatchBlock(\n  block: string,\n  handlers: {\n    onEvent: <E extends SseEventName>(event: E, payload: SsePayloadMap[E]) => void;\n    onError?: (payload: SsePayloadMap[\"error\"]) => void;\n  },\n): void {\n  let eventName = \"message\";\n  const dataLines: string[] = [];\n  for (const line of block.split(\"\\n\")) {\n    if (line.startsWith(\"event:\")) eventName = line.slice(6).trim();\n    else if (line.startsWith(\"data:\")) dataLines.push(line.slice(5).trim());\n  }\n  if (!dataLines.length) return;\n  let payload: unknown;\n  try {\n    payload = JSON.parse(dataLines.join(\"\\n\"));\n  } catch {\n    return; // 畸形负载直接丢弃，不打断后续事件\n  }\n  if (eventName === \"error\") {\n    handlers.onError?.(payload as SsePayloadMap[\"error\"]);\n    return;\n  }\n  const known = Object.keys({\n    \"qa.progress\": 1,\n    \"qa.citations\": 1,\n    \"qa.answer\": 1,\n    \"qa.done\": 1,\n    \"video.queued\": 1,\n    \"video.progress\": 1,\n    \"video.done\": 1,\n    \"video.failed\": 1,\n  }) as SseEventName[];\n  if ((known as string[]).includes(eventName)) {\n    handlers.onEvent(eventName as SseEventName, payload as never);\n  }\n}\n"}

9-4.5\" />\n    </>\n  ),\n};\n\nexport function Icon({ name, size = 16, ...rest }: IconProps) {\n  return (\n    <svg\n      width={size}\n      height={size}\n      viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\"\n {"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/lib/format.ts", "content": "/**\n * 展示格式化工具（DESIGN.md §8：元信息格式统一为「12 个来源 · 2024年4月21日」）。\n */\n\n/** RFC 3339 UTC → 「2024年4月21日」 */\nexport function formatDate(iso: string | null | undefined): string {\n  if (!iso) return \"\";\n  const d = new Date(iso);\n  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return \"\";\n  return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}年${d.getUTCMonth() + 1}月${d.getUTCDate()}日`;\n}\n\n/** RFC 3339 UTC → 「14:32」（UTC 时区，表格式对齐） */\nexport function formatTime(iso: string | null | undefined): string {\n  if (!iso) return \"\";\n  const d = new Date(iso);\n  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return \"\";\n  const hh = String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, \"0\");\n  const mm = String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, \"0\");\n  return `${hh}:${mm}`;\n}\n\n/** 0.0-1.0 → 「45%」 */\nexport function formatPercent(v: number): string {\n  return `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`;\n}\n"}
```

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/hooks/useWorkbench.ts", "content": "/**\n * 工作台状态中枢：文档库、历史问答、视频队列与当前问答流（SSE）。\n * 所有字段名对齐 docs/API_SPEC.md。\n */\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from \"react\";\nimport {\n  ApiError,\n  deleteDocument,\n  getQaRecord,\n  listDocuments,\n  listQaRecords,\n  listVideoTasks,\n  uploadDocument,\n} from \"../lib/api\";\nimport { askStream } from \"../lib/sse\";\nimport type { Citation, DocumentRecord, QaRecordSummary, VideoTask } from \"../types\";\n\nexport interface QaProgressState {\n  stage: \"retrieval\" | \"generation\";\n  message: string;\n  progress?: number;\n}\n\nexport interface Workbench {\n  documents: DocumentRecord[];\n  qaRecords: QaRecordSummary[];\n  videoTasks: VideoTask[];\n  runningCount: number;\n\n  currentQuestion: string;\n  answer: string;\n  citations: Citation[];\n  confidence: number | null;\n  refusalReason: string | null;\n  progress: QaProgressState | null;\n  isAsking: boolean;\n  /** 当前问答关联的视频任务（SSE 推进） */\n  activeVideoTaskId: number | null;\n  error: string | null;\n  selectedHistoryId: number | null;\n\n  refreshDocuments: () => Promise<void>;\n  upload: (file: File) => Promise<void>;\n  removeDocument: (docId: string) => Promise<void>;\n  ask: (question: string) => void;\n  selectHistory: (id: number) => Promise<void>;\n  clearError: () => void;\n}\n\nexport function useWorkbench(): Workbench {\n  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState<DocumentRecord[]>([]);\n  const [qaRecords, setQaRecords] = useState<QaRecordSummary[]>([]);\n  const [videoTasks, setVideoTasks] = useState<VideoTask[]>([]);\n  const [runningCount, setRunningCount] = useState(0);\n\n  const [currentQuestion, setCurrentQuestion] = useState(\"\");\n  const [answer, setAnswer] = useState(\"\");\n  const [citations, setCitations] = useState<Citation[]>([]);\n  const [confidence, setConfidence] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const [refusalReason, setRefusalReason] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<QaProgressState | null>(null);\n  const [isAsking, setIsAsking] = useState(false);\n  const [activeVideoTaskId, setActiveVideoTaskId] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [selectedHistoryId, setSelectedHistoryId] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const abortRef = useRef<{ abort: () => void } | null>(null);\n\n  const refreshDocuments = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const res = await listDocuments();\n      setDocuments(res.documents);\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : \"文档列表加载失败，请稍后重试。\");\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const refreshHistory = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const res = await listQaRecords();\n      setQaRecords(res.records);\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : \"历史问答加载失败，请稍后重试。\");\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const refreshVideoTasks = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const res = await listVideoTasks();\n      setVideoTasks(res.tasks);\n      setRunningCount(res.running_count);\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : \"视频任务列表加载失败，请稍后重试。\");\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  /** 按事件即时更新本地视频任务（不存在的任务先占位，随后全量刷新对齐） */\n  const patchVideoTask = useCallback(\n    (taskId: number, patch: Partial<VideoTask>) => {\n      setVideoTasks((prev) => {\n        if (!prev.some((t) => t.id === taskId)) {\n          // 占位：字段以 SSE 通报为准，其余从缺省\n          setRunningCount((n) => n); // 无操作，保持队列计数由全量刷新修正\n          return [\n            ...prev,\n            {\n              id: taskId,\n              qa_record_id: 0,\n              status: patch.status ?? \"queued\",\n              progress: patch.progress ?? 0,\n              queue_position: patch.queue_position,\n              stage: patch.stage,\n              video_url: patch.video_url ?? null,\n              error: patch.error ?? null,\n              created_at: \"\",\n              started_at: null,\n              finished_at: null,\n            },\n          ];\n        }\n        return prev.map((t) => (t.id === taskId ? { ...t, ...patch } : t));\n      });\n    },\n    [],\n  );\n\n  const ask = useCallback(\n    (question: string) => {\n      const trimmed = question.trim();\n      if (!trimmed) return;\n      // 重复提问前终止上一条流，避免双流交错\n      abortRef.current?.abort();\n\n      setIsAsking(true);\n      setError(null);\n      setCurrentQuestion(trimmed);\n      setAnswer(\"\");\n      setCitations([]);\n      setConfidence(null);\n      setRefusalReason(null);\n      setProgress({ stage: \"retrieval\", message: \"正在检索知识库…\" });\n      setSelectedHistoryId(null);\n\n      const session = askStream(\n        trimmed,\n        10,\n        {\n          onEvent: (event, payload) => {\n            switch (event) {\n              case \"qa.progress\":\n                setProgress({ stage: payload.stage, message: payload.message, progress: payload.progress });\n                break;\n              case \"qa.citations\":\n                setCitations(payload.citations);\n                break;\n              case \"qa.answer\":\n                setAnswer((prev) => prev + payload.delta);\n                break;\n              case \"qa.done\":\n                setActiveVideoTaskId(payload.video_task_id);\n                // 问答落库：刷新历史与队列全貌\n                void refreshHistory();\n                void refreshVideoTasks();\n                break;\n              case \"video.queued\":\n                patchVideoTask(payload.task_id, { status: \"queued\", queue_position: payload.queue_position, progress: 0 });\n                break;\n              case \"video.progress\":\n                patchVideoTask(payload.task_id, { status: \"running\", progress: payload.progress, stage: payload.stage });\n                break;\n              case \"video.done\":\n                patchVideoTask(payload.task_id, { status: \"done\", progress: 1, video_url: payload.video_url });\n                break;\n              case \"video.failed\":\n                patchVideoTask(payload.task_id, { status: \"failed\", error: payload.error });\n                break;\n              default:\n                break;\n            }\n          },\n          onError: (payload) => setError(`${payload.code}：${payload.message}`),\n          onClose: () => {\n            setIsAsking(false);\n            void refreshVideoTasks();\n            void refreshHistory();\n          },\n        },\n        // TS 分发器按事件名收窄 payload\n      ) as unknown as Parameters<typeof askStream>[2];\n      abortRef.current = session;\n    },\n    [patchVideoTask, refreshHistory, refreshVideoTasks],\n  );\n\n  const selectHistory = useCallback(async (id: number) => {\n    try {\n      const res = await getQaRecord(id);\n      const rec = res.record;\n      setSelectedHistoryId(id);\n      setCurrentQuestion(rec.question);\n      setAnswer(rec.answer);\n      setCitations(rec.citations);\n      setConfidence(rec.confidence);\n      setRefusalReason(rec.refusal_reason);\n      setProgress(null);\n      setIsAsking(false);\n      setActiveVideoTaskId(rec.video_task_id);\n      setError(null);\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : \"历史问答加载失败，请稍后重试。\");\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const upload = useCallback(async (file: File) => {\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      await uploadDocument(file);\n      await refreshDocuments();\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : \"文档入库失败，请检查文件格式后重试。\");\n    }\n  }, [refreshDocuments]);\n\n  const removeDocument = useCallback(\n    async (docId: string) => {\n      setError(null);\n      try {\n        await deleteDocument(docId);\n        await refreshDocuments();\n      } catch (err) {\n        setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : \"文档删除失败，请稍后重试。\");\n      }\n    },\n    [refreshDocuments],\n  );\n\n  // 挂载时拉取三份列表；卸载时终止未完成的 SSE 流\n  useEffect(() => {\n    void refreshDocuments();\n    void refreshHistory();\n    void refreshVideoTasks();\n    return () => abortRef.current?.abort();\n  }, [refreshDocuments, refreshHistory, refreshVideoTasks]);\n\n  return {\n    documents,\n    qaRecords,\n    videoTasks,\n    runningCount,\n    currentQuestion,\n    answer,\n    citations,\n    confidence,\n    refusalReason,\n    progress,\n    isAsking,\n    activeVideoTaskId,\n    error,\n    selectedHistoryId,\n    refreshDocuments,\n    upload,\n    removeDocument,\n    ask,\n    selectHistory,\n    clearError: () => setError(null),\n  };\n}\n"}
```

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/components/DocUploader.tsx", "content": "/**\n * 文档拖拽上传区（Step 2）：支持 .md / .docx / .doc；\n * 拖拽悬停整行填充 --nb-bg-hover；仅接受三类扩展名，其余给出完整句子提示。\n */\nimport { useRef, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Icon } from \"./Icon\";\n\nconst ACCEPTED = [\".md\", \".docx\", \".doc\"];\n\ninterface DocUploaderProps {\n  onUpload: (file: File) => Promise<void>;\n  disabled?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport function DocUploader({ onUpload, disabled }: DocUploaderProps) {\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n  const [dragOver, setDragOver] = useState(false);\n  const [rejectMsg, setRejectMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  const acceptFiles = (files: FileList | null) => {\n    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;\n    const file = files[0];\n    const ext = `.${file.name.split(\".\").pop()?.toLowerCase()}`;\n    if (!ACCEPTED.includes(ext)) {\n      setRejectMsg(`仅支持 .md / .docx / .doc 文档，${file.name} 不在支持范围内。`);\n      return;\n    }\n    setRejectMsg(null);\n    void onUpload(file);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ padding: \"0 var(--space-16) var(--space-8)\" }}>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        disabled={disabled}\n        onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}\n        onDragOver={(e) => {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          setDragOver(true);\n        }}\n        onDragLeave={() => setDragOver(false)}\n        onDrop={(e) => {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          setDragOver(false);\n          acceptFiles(e.dataTransfer.files);\n        }}\n        className=\"flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center transition-colors\"\n        style={{\n          minHeight: 96,\n          gap: \"var(--space-8)\",\n          border: \"1px dashed var(--nb-border-strong)\",\n          borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-sm)\",\n          backgroundColor: dragOver ? \"var(--nb-bg-hover)\" : \"var(--nb-bg-subtle)\",\n          cursor: disabled ? \"not-allowed\" : \"pointer\",\n          opacity: disabled ? 0.5 : 1,\n        }}\n      >\n        <Icon name=\"upload\" size={20} style={{ color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }} />\n        <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-secondary)\" }}>\n          拖拽文档到此处，或点击选择文件\n        </span>\n        <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>\n          支持 .md / .docx / .doc\n        </span>\n      </button>\n      <input\n        ref={inputRef}\n        type=\"file\"\n        accept=\".md,.docx,.doc\"\n        className=\"hidden\"\n        onChange={(e) => {\n          acceptFiles(e.target.files);\n          e.target.value = \"\";\n        }}\n      />\n      {rejectMsg ? (\n        <p role=\"alert\" style={{ marginTop: \"var(--space-8)\", fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\" }}>\n          {rejectMsg}\n        </p>\n      ) : null}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}
```

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/components/DocList.tsx", "content": "/**\n * 已入库文档列表（Step 2）：行高 36–40px、单行截断、悬停整行填充，\n * 右侧 12px 说明文字 + 删除图标按钮；含注入隔离标记。\n */\nimport { Icon } from \"./Icon\";\nimport type { DocumentRecord } from \"../types\";\n\ninterface DocListProps {\n  documents: DocumentRecord[];\n  onDelete: (docId: string) => Promise<void>;\n  busy?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport function DocList({ documents, onDelete, busy }: DocListProps) {\n  return (\n    <ul role=\"list\" aria-label=\"已入库文档\" style={{ padding: \"0 var(--space-8) var(--space-8)\" }}>\n      {documents.length === 0 ? (\n        <li\n          style={{\n            padding: \"var(--space-12) var(--space-8)\",\n            fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\",\n            color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          知识库暂无文档，上传 .md / .docx / .doc 文件后即可提问。\n        </li>\n      ) : (\n        documents.map((doc) => (\n          <li\n            key={doc.doc_id}\n            className=\"group flex items-center transition-colors\"\n            style={{\n              height: 40,\n              padding: \"0 var(--space-8)\",\n              gap: \"var(--space-8)\",\n              borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-sm)\",\n            }}\n            onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = \"var(--nb-bg-hover)\")}\n            onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = \"transparent\")}\n          >\n            <Icon name=\"file\" size={16} style={{ color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\", flexShrink: 0 }} />\n            <span\n              className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\"\n              style={{\n                fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\",\n                color: \"var(--nb-text-secondary)\",\n                whiteSpace: \"nowrap\",\n                overflow: \"hidden\",\n                textOverflow: \"ellipsis\",\n              }}\n              title={doc.title}\n            >\n              {doc.title}\n            </span>\n            {doc.injection_flag === 1 ? (\n              <span\n                className=\"rounded-sm\"\n                style={{\n                  fontSize: \"var(--text-micro-label-size)\",\n                  lineHeight: \"var(--text-micro-label-line-height)\",\n                  fontWeight: \"var(--text-micro-label-weight)\",\n                  letterSpacing: \"var(--text-micro-label-letter-spacing)\",\n                  padding: \"2px 6px\",\n                  backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-bg)\",\n                  color: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\",\n                }}\n                title=\"该文档含疑似越权指令文本，已隔离，不参与检索\"\n              >\n                已隔离\n              </span>\n            ) : null}\n            <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\", flexShrink: 0 }}>\n              {doc.char_count} 字\n            </span>\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              aria-label={`删除文档 ${doc.title}`}\n              disabled={busy}\n              onClick={() => void onDelete(doc.doc_id)}\n              className=\"flex items-center justify-center rounded-full opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100\"\n              style={{ width: 28, height: 28, flexShrink: 0 }}\n              onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = \"var(--nb-bg)\")}\n              onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = \"transparent\")}\n            >\n              <Icon name=\"trash\" size={16} style={{ color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }} />\n            </button>\n          </li>\n        ))\n      )}\n    </ul>\n  );\n}\n"}

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/components/HistoryList.tsx", "content": "/**\n * 历史问答记录列表（Step 2）：点击回看历史问答、引用与视频；\n * 行内显示关联视频状态徽标（文字 + 图标，不只用颜色，DESIGN.md §3）。\n */\nimport { Icon } from \"./Icon\";\nimport type { QaRecordSummary, VideoTaskStatus } from \"../types\";\nimport { formatDate, formatTime } from \"../lib/format\";\n\ninterface HistoryListProps {\n  records: QaRecordSummary[];\n  selectedId: number | null;\n  onSelect: (id: number) => Promise<void>;\n}\n\nconst STATUS_LABEL: Record<Exclude<VideoTaskStatus, null>, string> = {\n  queued: \"排队中\",\n  running: \"生成中\",\n  done: \"可播放\",\n  failed: \"失败\",\n};\n\nexport function HistoryList({ records, selectedId, onSelect }: HistoryListProps) {\n  return (\n    <ul role=\"list\" aria-label=\"历史问答\" style={{ padding: \"0 var(--space-8) var(--space-8)\" }}>\n      {records.length === 0 ? (\n        <li\n          style={{\n            padding: \"var(--space-12) var(--space-8)\",\n            fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\",\n            color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          还没有问答记录，提出第一个问题后会自动保存在这里。\n        </li>\n      ) : (\n        records.map((rec) => {\n          const active = rec.id === selectedId;\n          return (\n            <li key={rec.id}>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => void onSelect(rec.id)}\n                aria-current={active ? \"true\" : undefined}\n                className=\"flex w-full items-start transition-colors\"\n                style={{\n                  width: \"100%\",\n                  minHeight: 40,\n                  padding: \"var(--space-8)\",\n                  gap: \"var(--space-8)\",\n                  textAlign: \"left\",\n                  borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-sm)\",\n                  backgroundColor: active ? \"var(--nb-bg-hover)\" : \"transparent\",\n                }}\n              >\n                <Icon\n                  name=\"history\"\n                  size={16}\n                  style={{ color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\", flexShrink: 0, marginTop: 2 }}\n                />\n                <span className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                  <span\n                    style={{\n                      display: \"block\",\n                      fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\",\n                      color: active ? \"var(--nb-text)\" : \"var(--nb-text-secondary)\",\n                      whiteSpace: \"nowrap\",\n                      overflow: \"hidden\",\n                      textOverflow: \"ellipsis\",\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    {rec.question}\n                  </span>\n                  <span\n                    className=\"flex items-center\"\n                    style={{\n                      marginTop: 2,\n                      gap: \"var(--space-8)\",\n                      fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\",\n                      color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\",\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    <span>\n                      {formatDate(rec.created_at)} {formatTime(rec.created_at)}\n                    </span>\n                    {rec.video_status ? (\n                      <span className=\"flex items-center\" style={{ gap: 4 }}>\n                        <VideoStatusDot status={rec.video_status} />\n                        {STATUS_LABEL[rec.video_status]}\n                      </span>\n                    ) : null}\n                  </span>\n                </span>\n              </button>\n            </li>\n          );\n        })\n      )}\n    </ul>\n  );\n}\n\n/** 状态圆点：颜色是信号之一，始终配文字（DESIGN.md §3 规则） */\nfunction VideoStatusDot({ status }: { status: VideoTaskStatus }) {\n  const map: Record<VideoTaskStatus, { bg: string; ink: string }> = {\n    queued: { bg: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-bg)\", ink: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\" },\n    running: { bg: \"var(--nb-tint-blue-bg)\", ink: \"var(--nb-tint-blue-ink)\" },\n    done: { bg: \"var(--nb-tint-green-bg)\", ink: \"var(--nb-tint-green-ink)\" },\n    failed: { bg: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-bg)\", ink: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\" },\n  };\n  return (\n    <span\n      aria-hidden=\"true\"\n      style={{\n        width: 8,\n        height: 8,\n        borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-pill)\",\n        backgroundColor: map[status].bg,\n        boxShadow: `inset 0 0 0 1px ${map[status].ink}`,\n        flexShrink: 0,\n      }}\n    />\n  );\n}\n"}

{\n    if (!value.trim() || disabled) return;\n    onAsk(value);\n    setValue(\"\");\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: \"flex\", flexDirection: \"column\", gap: \"var(--space-12)\"     onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}\n          onKeyDown={(e) => {\n            if (e.key === \"Enter\") submit();\n          }}\n          placeholder=\"基于知识库提问，答案将附引用溯源…\"\n \"var(--nb-text)\",\n          }}\n        />\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          onClick={submit}\n          disabled={disabled || !value.trim()}\n          className=\"rounded-sm transition-colors\"\n          style={{\n            height: 32,\n            padding: \"0 var(--space-16)\",\n            border: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border-strong)\",\n            backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg)\",\n            color: value.trim() ? \"var(--nb-text)\" : \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\",\n            fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\",\n            fontWeight:    </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {examples.length > 0 ? ` · ${formatDate(createdAt)}` : \"\"}\n        </span>\n        <span>点击卡片查看原文段落</span>\n      </div>\n\n      <div\n        className=\"flex\"\n        style={{ gap: \"var(--space-8)\", overflowX: \"auto\", paddingBottom: \"var(--space-4)\" }}\n        role=\"listbox\"\n        aria-label=\"引用来源卡片\"\n      >\n        {citations.map((cit, i) => {\n          const isActive = selected === i;\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={`${cit.para_id}-${i}`}\n              type=\"button\"\n              role=\"option\"\n              aria-selected={isActive}\n              onClick={() => setSelected(isActive ? null : i)}\n              className=\"flex min-w-0 transition-colors\"\n              style={{\n                width: 240,\n                flexShrink: 0,\n                padding: \"var(--space-12)\",\n                gap: \"var(--space-12)\",\n                border: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\",\n                borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-md)\",\n                backgroundColor: isActive ? \"var(--nb-accent-soft)\" : \"var(--nb-bg)\",\n                textAlign: \"left\",\n                cursor: \"pointer\",\n              }}\n            >\n              <span\n                className=\"flex items-center justify-center\"\n                style={{\n                  width: 32,\n                  height: 32,\n                  flexShrink: 0,\n                  borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-sm)\",\n                  backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-tint-blue-bg)\",\n                  color: \"var(--nb-tint-blue-ink)\",\n                }}\n              >\n                <Icon name=\"file\" size={16} />\n              </span>\n              <span className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n                <span\n                  style={{\n                    display: \"block\",\n                    fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\",\n                    fontWeight: 500,\n                    lineHeight: \"var(--text-body-line-height)\",\n                    color: \"var(--nb-text)\",\n                    whiteSpace: \"nowrap\",\n                    overflow: \"hidden\",\n                    textOverflow: \"ellipsis\",\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {cit.source_file}\n                </span>\n                <span\n                  style={{\n                    display: \"block\",\n                    marginTop: 2,\n                    fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\",\n                    lineHeight: \"var(--text-meta-line-height)\",\n                    color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\",\n                    whiteSpace: \"nowrap\",\n                    overflow: \"hidden\",\n                    textOverflow: \"ellipsis\",\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {cit.heading_path}\n                </span>\n              </span>\n              <Icon\n                name={isActive ? \"chevron-down\" : \"chevron-right\"}\n                size={16}\n                style={{ color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\", flexShrink: 0, alignSelf: \"center\" }}\n              />\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n\n      {active ? (\n        <div\n          className=\"flex flex-col\"\n          style={{\n            gap: \"var(--space-8)\",\n            padding: \"var(--space-16)\",\n            border: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\",\n            borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-md)\",\n            backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-baseline\" style={{ gap: \"var(--space-12)\" }}>\n            <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-card-title-size-zh)\", fontWeight: 500, color: \"var(--nb-text)\" }}>\n              {active.doc_title}\n            </span>\n            <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>\n              原文文件：{active.source_file}\n            </span>\n            <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>\n              语料编号：{active.doc_id}\n            </span>\n            <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>\n              相似度 {(active.score * 100).toFixed(0)}%\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>\n            章节路径：{active.heading_path}\n          </div>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>\n            段落定位：{active.para_id}\n          </div>\n          <blockquote\n            style={{\n              margin: 0,\n              padding:   }}\n          >\n            {active.snippet}\n          </blockquote>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n"}

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/components/AnswerView.tsx", "content": "/**\n * 答案区（Step 3）：SSE 检索/生成阶段状态 + 流式正文 + 拒答/错误态。\n * 空状态、加载态、错误态均为完整句子（DESIGN.md §8）。\n */\nimport { Icon } from \"./Icon\";\nimport type { QaProgressState } from \"../hooks/useWorkbench\";\n\ninterface AnswerViewProps {\n  question: string;\n  answer: string;\n  progress: QaProgressState | null;\n  isAsking: boolean;\n  refusalReason: string | null;\n  confidence: number | null;\n  error: string | null;\n}\n\nconst STAGE_LABEL = { retrieval: \"检索阶段\", generation: \"生成阶段\" } as const;\n\nexport function AnswerView({ question, answer, progress, isAsking, refusalReason, confidence, error }: AnswerViewProps) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col\" style={{ gap: \"var(--space-16)\", minHeight: 200 }}>\n      {question ? (\n        <div>\n          <div\n            style={{\n              fontSize: \"var(--text-micro-label-size)\",\n              lineHeight: \"var(--text-micro-label-line-height)\",\n              fontWeight: \"var(--text-micro-label-weight)\",\n              letterSpacing: \"var(--text-micro-label-letter-spacing)\",\n              color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\",\n              marginBottom: \"var(--space-4)\",\n            }}\n          >\n            问题\n          </div>\n          <p style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-card-title-size-zh)\", fontWeight: 500, color: \"var(--nb-text)\", lineHeight: 1.45 }}>\n            {question}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      {error ? (\n        <div\n          role=\"alert\"\n          className=\"flex items-start\"\n          style={{\n            gap: \"var(--space-8)\",\n            padding: \"var(--space-16)\",\n            border: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\",\n            borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-md)\",\n            backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg-subtle)\",\n            color: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\",\n            fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          <Icon name=\"alert\" size={16} style={{ marginTop: 3, flexShrink: 0 }} />\n          <span>{error}</span>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      {progress && isAsking ? (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center\" style={{ gap: \"var(--space-12)\" }}>\n          <Icon name=\"spinner\" size={16} style={{ color: \"var(--nb-accent)\", animation: \"nb-spin 1s linear infinite\" }} />\n          <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-secondary)\" }}>\n            {STAGE_LABEL[progress.stage]}：{progress.message}\n            {typeof progress.progress === \"number\" ? `（${Math.round(progress.progress * 100)}%）` : \"\"}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      {refusalReason ? (\n        <div\n          className=\"flex items-start\"\n          style={{\n            gap: \"var(--space-8)\",\n            padding: \"var(--space-16)\",\n            border: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\",\n            borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-md)\",\n            backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg-subtle)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          <Icon name=\"alert\" size={16} style={{ color: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\", marginTop: 3, flexShrink: 0 }} />\n          <div>\n            <p style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-secondary)\", margin: 0 }}>{answer || \"文档未给出确定数值，无法回答该问题。\"}</p>\n            <p style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\", margin: \"var(--space-8) 0 0\" }}>\n              拒答原因：{refusalReason}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ) : answer ? (\n        <p\n          style={{\n            margin: 0,\n            fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size-zh, 15px)\",\n            lineHeight: 1.75,\n            color: \"var(--nb-text-secondary)\",\n            whiteSpace: \"pre-wrap\",\n          }}\n        >\n          {answer}\n          {isAsking ? <span style={{ color: \"var(--nb-accent)\" }}>▍</span> : null}\n        </p>\n      ) : !isAsking && !error ? (\n        <p style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\", margin: 0 }}>\n          在上方输入问题并点击「提问」，系统将基于已入库文档作答，并附上引用来源与视频生成进度。\n        </p>\n      ) : null}\n\n      {typeof confidence === \"number\" && !refusalReason ? (\n        <div style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>\n          可信度 {(confidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      <style>{`@keyframes nb-spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }`}</style>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/components/VideoPanel.tsx", "content": "/**\n * 视频工作室（Step 4）：当前问答的 MP4 播放器 + 下方视频任务队列面板。\n * 队列严格按 DESIGN.md §3 成对功能色块 + 线性图标展示四种状态：\n * queued（排队位次与前方人数）、running/processing（实时进度条）、\n * done/completed（可播放）、failed（失败原因）。\n * 全局单并发：running_count ≤ 1（API_SPEC §6.2）。\n */\nimport { Icon } from \"./Icon\";\nimport type { VideoTask } from \"../types\";\nimport { formatPercent } from \"../lib/format\";\n\ninterface VideoPanelProps {\n  tasks: VideoTask[];\n  runningCount: number;\n  activeTaskId: number | null;\n}\n\n/** 成对功能色块：底浅一档、墨深一档（DESIGN.md §3） */\nconst STATUS_TINT: Record<VideoTask[\"status\"], { bg: string; ink: string; icon: \"queue\" | \"spinner\" | \"play\" | \"alert\" }> = {\n  queued: { bg: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-bg)\", ink: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\", icon: \"queue\" },\n  running: { bg: \"var(--nb-tint-blue-bg)\", ink: \"var(--nb-tint-blue-ink)\", icon: \"spinner\" },\n  done: { bg: \"var(--nb-tint-green-bg)\", ink: \"var(--nb-tint-green-ink)\", icon: \"play\" },\n  failed: { bg: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-bg)\", ink: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\", icon: \"alert\" },\n};\n\nconst STAGE_LABEL: Record<NonNullable<VideoTask[\"stage\"]>, string> = {\n  script: \"脚本\",\n  tts: \"语音合成\",\n  render: \"画面渲染\",\n  mux: \"音画合成\",\n};\n\nconst STATUS_LABEL: Record<VideoTask[\"status\"], string> = {\n  queued: \"排队中\",\n  running: \"生成中\",\n  done: \"已完成\",\n  failed: \"失败\",\n};\n\nexport function VideoPanel({ tasks, runningCount, activeTaskId }: VideoPanelProps) {\n  const active = tasks.find((t) => t.id === activeTaskId) ?? null;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col\" style={{ gap: \"var(--space-16)\" }}>\n      {/* 当前问答视频播放器 */}\n      <section aria-label=\"当前问答视频\">\n        {active?.status === \"done\" && active.video_url ? (\n          <video\n            controls\n            src={active.video_url}\n            preload=\"metadata\"\n            style={{ width: \"100%\", height: \"auto\", borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-md)\", border: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\" }}\n          >\n            您的浏览器不支持视频播放，请下载后观看。\n          </video>\n        ) : (\n          <div\n            className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center\"\n            style={{\n              aspectRatio: \"16 / 9\",\n              border: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\",\n              borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-md)\",\n              backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg-subtle)\",\n              gap: \"var(--space-8)\",\n              padding: \"var(--space-16)\",\n              textAlign: \"center\",\n            }}\n          >\n            <Icon name=\"film\" size={24} style={{ color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }} />\n            <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>\n              {active?.status === \"running\"\n                ? \"视频正在合成，完成后即可在此播放。\"\n                : active?.status === \"queued\"\n                  ? \"视频正在排队，前方任务合成完成后开始生成。\"\n                  : active?.status === \"failed\"\n                    ? \"本次视频合成失败，可重新提问生成新任务。\"\n                    : \"提问后系统将自动生成问答视频。\"}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </section>\n\n      {/* 队列全貌 */}\n      <div\n        className=\"flex items-center justify-between\"\n        style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}\n      >\n        <span>任务队列 · 单并发</span>\n        <span>\n          运行中 {runningCount} / 1 · 共 {tasks.length} 条\n        </span>\n      </div>\n\n      <ul role=\"list\" aria-label=\"视频任务队列\" style={{ display: \"flex\", flexDirection: \"column\", gap: \"var(--space-8)\", margin: 0, padding: 0, listStyle: \"none\" }}>\n        {tasks.length === 0 ? (\n          <li style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\", padding: \"var(--space-8) 0\" }}>\n            暂无视频任务，提出第一个问题后自动创建。\n          </li>\n        ) : (\n          tasks.map((task) => (\n            <VideoTaskRow key={task.id} task={task} isActive={task.id === activeTaskId} />\n          ))\n        )}\n      </ul>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction VideoTaskRow({ task, isActive }: { task: VideoTask; isActive: boolean }) {\n  const tint = STATUS_TINT[task.status];\n  const line1 =\n    task.status === \"queued\"\n      ? `排队中 · 前方还有 ${(task.queue_position ?? 1) - 1} 个任务`\n      : task.status === \"running\"\n        ? `生成中 · ${task.stage ? `${STAGE_LABEL[task.stage]}阶段 · ` : \"\"}${formatPercent(task.progress)}`\n        : task.status === \"done\"\n          ? \"已完成 · 可播放\"\n          : `失败 · ${task.error ?? \"未知错误\"}`;\n\n  return (\n    <li\n      style={{\n        display: \"flex\",\n        gap: \"var(--space-12)\",\n        alignItems: \"flex-start\",\n        padding: \"var(--space-12)\",\n        border: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\",\n        borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-md)\",\n        backgroundColor: isActive ? \"var(--nb-bg-hover)\" : \"var(--nb-bg)\",\n      }}\n    >\n      <span\n        className=\"flex items-center justify-center\"\n        style={{\n          width: 32,\n          height: 32,\n          flexShrink: 0,\n          borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-sm)\",\n          backgroundColor: tint.bg,\n          color: tint.ink,\n        }}\n      >\n        <Icon name={tint.icon} size={16} style={task.status === \"running\" ? { animation: \"nb-spin 1s linear infinite\" } : undefined} />\n      </span>\n      <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-baseline justify-between\" style={{ gap: \"var(--space-8)\" }}>\n          <span style={{ fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\", fontWeight: 500, color: \"var(--nb-text)\" }}>\n            视频任务 #{task.id}\n            {isActive ? \" · 当前\" : \"\"}\n          </span>\n          <span\n            className=\"flex items-center\"\n            style={{ gap: 4, fontSize: \"var(--text-micro-label-size)\", fontWeight: 500, letterSpacing: \"var(--text-micro-label-letter-spacing)\", color: tint.ink, flexShrink: 0 }}\n          >\n            <span\n              aria-hidden=\"true\"\n              style={{ width: 6, height: 6, borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-pill)\", backgroundColor: tint.ink }}\n            />\n            {STATUS_LABEL[task.status]}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n        <div style={{ marginTop: 2, fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-text-tertiary)\" }}>{line1}</div>\n        {task.status === \"running\" ? (\n          <div\n            role=\"progressbar\"\n            aria-valuenow={Math.round(task.progress * 100)}\n            aria-valuemin={0}\n            aria-valuemax={100}\n            aria-label={`任务 #${task.id} 合成进度`}\n            style={{ marginTop: \"var(--space-8)\", height: 4, borderRadius: \"var(--nb-radius-pill)\", backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg-subtle)\", overflow: \"hidden\" }}\n          >\n            <div\n              style={{\n                width: `${Math.min(100, Math.round(task.progress * 100))}%`,\n                height: \"100%\",\n                backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-accent)\",\n                transition: \"width var(--duration-normal) var(--ease-out)\",\n              }}\n            />\n          </div>\n        ) : null}\n        {task.status === \"done\" && task.video_url ? (\n          <a\n            href={task.video_url}\n            download\n            className=\"flex items-center\"\n            style={{ marginTop: \"var(--space-8)\", fontSize: \"var(--text-meta-size)\", color: \"var(--nb-accent)\", gap: 4, textDecoration: \"none\" }}\n          >\n            <Icon name=\"play\" size={12} />\n            播放 / 下载视频\n          </a>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n    </li>\n  );\n}\n"}

{"file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/App.tsx", "content": "/**\n * 编辑型工作台三栏布局（DESIGN.md §5）：\n * 顶栏 56px；左栏 260px（可折叠）、中栏 max-width 720px 居中、右栏 300px。\n * 层次靠 1px 描边与留白承担，无阴影、无渐变、无面性图标。\n */\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { TopBar } from \"./components/TopBar\";\nimport { Sidebar } from \"./components/Sidebar\";\nimport { MainPanel } from \"./components/MainPanel\";\nimport { VideoPanel } from \"./components/VideoPanel\";\nimport { useWorkbench } from \"./hooks/useWorkbench\";\nimport type { VideoTask } from \"./types\";\n\nconst EXAMPLE_QUESTIONS = [\n  \"众赢科技 2025 年研发投入占营收比重是多少？\",\n  \"澎湃教育的主营业务有哪些？\",\n  \"未来在线的信息技术服务面向哪些行业？\",\n];\n\nexport default function App() {\n  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(true);\n  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);\n  const wb = useWorkbench();\n\n  const activeVideoTaskId = wb.activeVideoTaskId;\n  const sortedTasks: VideoTask[] = [...wb.videoTasks].sort((a, b) => {\n    const rank = (t: VideoTask) => (t.status === \"running\" ? 0 : t.status === \"queued\" ? 1 : 2);\n    const r = rank(a) - rank(b);\n    if (r !== 0) return r;\n    return b.id - a.id;\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{\n        minHeight: \"100vh\",\n        backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg)\",\n        color: \"var(--nb-text)\",\n        fontFamily: \"var(--font-family-sans)\",\n        display: \"flex\",\n        flexDirection: \"column\",\n      }}\n    >\n      <TopBar\n        sidebarOpen={sidebarOpen}\n        onToggleSidebar={() => setSidebarOpen((v) => !v)}\n        onCreate={() => wb.ask(EXAMPLE_QUESTIONS[0])}\n      />\n\n      {wb.error ? (\n        <div\n          role=\"alert\"\n          className=\"flex items-center justify-between\"\n          style={{\n            padding: \"var(--space-8) var(--space-16)\",\n            backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-bg)\",\n            color: \"var(--nb-tint-amber-ink)\",\n            borderBottom: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\",\n            fontSize: \"var(--text-body-size)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          <span>{wb.error}</span>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            aria-label=\"关闭错误提示\"\n            className=\"flex items-center justify-center rounded-full\"\n            style={{ width: 28, height: 28 }}\n            onClick={wb.clearError}\n          >\n            <Icon name=\"close\" size={16} />\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      <div className=\"flex min-h-0 flex-1\" style={{ height: \"calc(100vh - 56px)\" }}>\n        {/* 左栏 260px：文档库 + 历史问答 */}\n        <aside\n          style={{\n            width: sidebarOpen ? 260 : 0,\n            minWidth: sidebarOpen ? 260 : 0,\n            overflow: \"hidden\",\n            transition: \"width var(--duration-normal) var(--ease-out)\",\n            backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg-subtle)\",\n            borderRight: sidebarOpen ? \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\" : \"none\",\n          }}\n        >\n          <div style={{ width: 260, height: \"100%\", padding: \"var(--space-16)\" }}>\n            <Sidebar\n              documents={wb.documents}\n              qaRecords={wb.qaRecords}\n              selectedHistoryId={wb.selectedHistoryId}\n              onUpload={async (f) => {\n                setUploading(true);\n                try {\n                  await wb.upload(f);\n                } finally {\n                  setUploading(false);\n                }\n              }}\n              onDeleteDoc={wb.removeDocument}\n              onSelectHistory={wb.selectHistory}\n              uploading={uploading}\n            />\n          </div>\n        </aside>\n\n        {/* 中栏：问答主区，max-width 720px 居中 */}\n        <main\n          className=\"flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-start justify-center overflow-y-auto\"\n          style={{ padding: \"var(--space-24)\" }}\n        >\n          <MainPanel\n            question={wb.currentQuestion}\n            answer={wb.answer}\n            citations={wb.citations}\n            progress={wb.progress}\n            isAsking={wb.isAsking}\n            refusalReason={wb.refusalReason}\n            confidence={wb.confidence}\n            error={null}\n            onAsk={wb.ask}\n            examples={EXAMPLE_QUESTIONS}\n          />\n        </main>\n\n        {/* 右栏 300px：视频工作室 */}\n        <aside\n          style={{\n            width: 300,\n            minWidth: 300,\n            backgroundColor: \"var(--nb-bg-subtle)\",\n            borderLeft: \"var(--border-width) solid var(--nb-border)\",\n            padding: \"var(--space-16)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          <div\n            style={{\n              fontSize: \"var(--text-section-title-size)\",\n              lineHeight: \"var(--text-section-title-line-height)\",\n              fontWeight: \"var(--text-section-title-weight)\",\n              color: \"var(--nb-text)\",\n              marginBottom: \"var(--space-8)\",\n            }}\n          >\n            视频工作室\n          </div>\n          <VideoPanel tasks={sortedTasks} runningCount={wb.runningCount} activeTaskId={activeVideoTaskId} />\n        </aside>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

1	/**
2	 * SSE 客户端 —— 事件名与负载字段 100% 对齐 docs/API_SPEC.md §6。
3	 * POST /api/ask 返回 text/event-stream：event: <type>\ndata: <json>\n\n
4	 */
5	import type { SseEventName, SsePayloadMap } from "../types";
6	
7	export interface SseSession {
8	  /** 主动终止流（组件卸载 / 重复提问时调用） */
9	  abort: () => void;
10	}
11	
12	/**
13	 * 发起一次问答 SSE 流。onEvent 按事件名分发（各事件 payload 类型由 API_SPEC §6 锁定）；
14	 * onError 收到 `error` 事件负载；流关闭（done 或异常）后调用 onClose。
15	 */
16	export function askStream(
17	  question: string,
18	  historyLimit: number,
19	  handlers: {
20	    onEvent: {
21	      "qa.progress": (p: SsePayloadMap["qa.progress"]) => void;
22	      "qa.citations": (p: SsePayloadMap["qa.citations"]) => void;
23	      "qa.answer": (p: SsePayloadMap["qa.answer"]) => void;
24	      "qa.done": (p: SsePayloadMap["qa.done"]) => void;
25	      "video.queued": (p: SsePayloadMap["video.queued"]) => void;
26	      "video.progress": (p: SsePayloadMap["video.progress"]) => void;
27	      "video.done": (p: SsePayloadMap["video.done"]) => void;
28	      "video.failed": (p: SsePayloadMap["video.failed"]) => void;
29	    };
30	    onError?: (payload: SsePayloadMap["error"]) => void;
31	    onClose?: () => void;
32	  },
33	): SseSession {
34	  const controller = new AbortController();
35	
36	  (async () => {
37	    let res: Response;
38	    try {
39	      res = await fetch("/api/ask", {
40	        method: "POST",
41	        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
42	        body: JSON.stringify({ question, history_limit: historyLimit }),
43	        signal: controller.signal,
44	      });
45	    } catch (err) {
46	      if ((err as Error).name !== "AbortError") handlers.onClose?.();
47	      return;
48	    }
49	    if (!res.ok || !res.body) {
50	      handlers.onError?.({ code: `E_HTTP_${res.status}`, message: res.statusText });
51	      handlers.onClose?.();
52	      return;
53	    }
54

{"replace_all": false, "file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/lib/sse.ts", "old_string": "function dispatchBlock(\n  block: string,\n  handlers: {\n    onEvent: <E extends SseEventName>(event: E, payload: SsePayloadMap[E]) => void;\n    onError?: (payload: SsePayloadMap[\"error\"]) => void;\n  },\n): void {\n  let eventName = \"message\";\n  const dataLines: string[] = [];\n  for (const line of block.split(\"\\n\")) {\n    if (line.startsWith(\"event:\")) eventName = line.slice(6).trim();\n    else if (line.startsWith(\"data:\")) dataLines.push(line.slice(5).trim());\n  }\n  if (!dataLines.length) return;\n  let payload: unknown;\n  try {\n    payload = JSON.parse(dataLines.join(\"\\n\"));\n  } catch {\n    return; 且不再分发给 onEvent\", async () => {\n    const errors: string[] = [];\n    const events: string[] = [];\n    vi.stubGlobal(\n      \"fetch\",\n      vi.fn(async () => streamOf('event: error\\ndata: {\"code\":\"E_LLM_FAILED\",\"message\":\"模型调用失败\"}\\n\\n')),\n    );\n\n    askStream(\"q\", 10, {\n      onEvent: (e) => events.push(e),\n      onError: (p) => errors.push(`${p.code}:${p.message}`),\n    });\n\n    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(errors).toEqual([\"E_LLM_FAILED:模型调用失败\"]));\n    expect(events).toEqual([]);\n    vi.unstubAllGlobals();\n  });\n\n  it(\"畸形 JSON 负载直接丢弃，不打断后续事件\", async () => {\n    const calls: string[] = [];\n    vi.stubGlobal(\n      \"fetch\",\n      vi.fn(async () =>\n        streamOf('event: qa.answer\\ndata: {不是 json}\\n\\nevent: qa.answer\\ndata: {\"delta\":\"正常\"}\\n\\n'),\n      ),\n    );\n\n    askStream(\"q\", 10, {\n      onEvent: (e, p) => {\n        calls.push(e);\n        if (e === \"qa.answer\") expect(typeof p.delta).toBe(\"string\");\n      },\n    });\n\n    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(calls.length).toBe(1));\n    expect(calls).toEqual([\"qa.answer\"]);\n    vi.unstubAllGlobals();\n  });\n\n  it(\"请求失败（非 200）走 onError 并 onClose\", async () => {\n    const errors: string[] = [];\n    const closed = vi.fn();\n    vi.stubGlobal(\"fetch\", vi.fn(async () => new Response(null, { status: 500, statusText: \"Internal Server Error\" })));\n\n    askStream(\"q\", 10, {\n      onEvent: () => {},\n      onError: (p) => errors.push(p.code),\n      onClose: closed,\n    });\n\n    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(closed).toHaveBeenCalledOnce());\n    expect(errors[0]).toMatch(/^E_HTTP_500/);\n    vi.unstubAllGlobals();\n  });\n});\n"}

*/\nimport { act, renderHook, waitFor } from \"@testing-library/react\";\nimport { describe, expect, it, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from \"vitest\";\nimport { useWorkbench } from \"./useWorkbench\";\n\nconst QA_SUMMARY = {\n  id: 1,\n  question: \"众赢科技 2025 年研发投入占营收比重是多少？\",\n  answer_summary: \"超过 75%。\",\n  video_task_id: 3,\n  video_status: \"done\" as const,\n  created_at: \"2026-11-18T10:00:00Z\",\n};\n\nconst QA_DETAIL = {\n  ...QA_SUMMARY,\n  answer: \"2025 年众赢科技研发投入达 11 亿元，占营业收入比重超过 75%。\",\n  refusal_reason: null,\n  confidence: 0.92,\n  citations: [\n    {\n      doc_id: \"doc05\",\n      doc_title: \"西海市众赢科技有限公司介绍\",\n      source_file: \"西海市众赢科技有限公司介绍.docx\",\n      heading_path: \"西海市众赢科技有限公司介绍 > 五、研发体系与投入\",\n      para_id: \"doc05#p041\",\n      snippet: \"2025 年，公司研发投入 11 亿元…\",\n      score: 0.87,\n    },\n  ],\n};\n\nfunction sseStream(events: string[]) {\n  const body = events.map((e) => `event: ${e.name}\\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(e.data)}\\n\\n`).join(\"\");\n  return new Response(body, { headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"text/event-stream\" } });\n}\n\nconst EVENTS = [\n  { name: \"qa.progress\", data: { stage: \"retrieval\", message: \"正在检索知识库…\", progress: 0.1 } },\n  {\n    name: \"qa.citations\",\n    data: { citations: QA_DETAIL.citations },\n  },\n  { name: \"qa.answer\", data: { delta: \"2025 年众赢科技研发投入达 11 亿元，\" } },\n  { name: \"qa.answer\", data: { delta: \"占营业收入比重超过 75%。\" } },\n  { name: \"qa.done\", data: { qa_record_id: 1, video_task_id: 3 } },\n  { name: \"video.queued\", data: { task_id: 3, queue_position: 1 } },\n  { name: \"video.progress\", data: { task_id: 3, progress: 0.5, stage: \"tts\" } },\n  { name: \"video.done\", data: { task_id: 3, video_url: \"/api/videos/3/file\" } },\n];\n\ndescribe(\"useWorkbench 历史切换与 SSE 实时更新\", () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    vi.stubGlobal(\n      \"fetch\",\n      vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL) => {\n        const url = String(input);\n        if (url === \"/api/documents\") return Response.json({ documents: [], total: 0 });\n        if (url === \"/api/qa\") return Response.json({ records: [QA_SUMMARY], total: 1 });\n        if (url === \"/api/qa/1\") return Response.json({ record: QA_DETAIL });\n        if (url === \"/api/video-tasks\") return Response.json({ tasks: [], running_count: 0 });\n        if (url === \"/api/ask\") return sseStream(EVENTS);\n        return new Response(null, { status: 404 });\n      }),\n    );\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());\n\n  it(\"挂载时加载历史问答，点击历史条目回看完整问答与引用\", async () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useWorkbench());\n    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.qaRecords).toHaveLength(1));\n    expect(result.current.qaRecords[0].question).toBe(QA_SUMMARY.question);\n\n    await act(() => result.current.selectHistory(1));\n\n    expect(result.current.selectedHistoryId).toBe(1);\n    expect(result.current.currentQuestion).toBe(QA_DETAIL.question ?? QA_SUMMARY.question);\n    expect(result.current.answer).toBe(QA_DETAIL.answer);\n    expect(result.current.citations).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(result.current.citations[0].para_id).toBe(\"doc05#p041\");\n    expect(result.current.citations[0].heading_path).toContain(\"五、研发体系与投入\");\n    expect(result.current.confidence).toBe(0.92);\n    expect(result.current.activeVideoTaskId).toBe(3);\n  });\n\n  it(\"SSE 流实时推进：进度 → 引用 → 流式回答 → 视频队列四态\", async () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useWorkbench());\n    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.qaRecords).toHaveLength(1));\n\n    act(() => result.current.ask(\"众赢科技 2025 年研发投入占营收比重是多少？\"));\n    expect(result.current.isAsking).toBe(true);\n    expect(result.current.currentQuestion).toBe(\"众赢科技 2025 年研发投入占营收比重是多少？\");\n\n    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.isAsking).toBe(false));\n\n    // 引用已渲染\n    expect(result.current.citations).toHaveLength(1);\n    // 流式回答拼合完整\n    expect(result.current.answer).toBe(\"2025 年众赢科技研发投入达 11 亿元，占营业收入比重超过 75%。\");\n    // 关联视频任务\n    expect(result.current.activeVideoTaskId).toBe(3);\n    // 视频任务经 SSE 推进到 done（qa.done 刷新后由 video.* 事件补丁）\n    await waitFor(() =>\n      expect(result.current.videoTasks.some((t) => t.id === 3 && t.status === \"done\")).toBe(true),\n    );\n    const task = result.current.videoTasks.find((t) => t.id === 3)!;\n    expect(task.video_url).toBe(\"/api/videos/3/file\");\n  }, 15000);\n\n  it(\"重复提问前终止上一条 SSE 流（abort）\", async () => {\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useWorkbench());\n    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.qaRecords).toHaveLength(1));\n\n    const abortSpy = vi.fn();\n    let captured: { abort: () => void } | null = null;\n    vi.stubGlobal(\n      \"fetch\",\n      vi.fn(async (input: RequestInfo | URL) => {\n        const url = String(input);\n        if (url === \"/api/ask\") {\n          const controller = new AbortController();\n          captured = { abort: () => abortSpy() };\n          void controller;\n          return sseStream([EVENTS[0]]);\n        }\n        if (url === \"/api/documents\") return Response.json({ documents: [], total: 0 });\n        if (url === \"/api/qa\") return Response.json({ records: [QA_SUMMARY], total: 1 });\n        if (url === \"/api/qa/1\") return Response.json({ record: QA_DETAIL });\n        if (url === \"/api/video-tasks\") return Response.json({ tasks: [], running_count: 0 });\n        return new Response(null, { status: 404 });\n      }),\n    );\n\n    act(() => result.current.ask(\"问题一\"));\n    act(() => result.current.ask(\"问题二\"));\n    // 两次提问产生两次流会话\n    expect(captured).not.toBeNull();\n    expect(result.current.currentQuestion).toBe(\"问题二\");\n  });\n});\n"}

## [Claude] 2026-11-18T16:01:58.680Z

根因明确：`qa.done` / `onClose` 的全量刷新会用服务器旧状态覆盖 SSE 刚补丁的本地任务（SSE 才是实时权威）。修复为合并式刷新（本地已有任务不被回退）：

---

## [Claude] 2026-11-18T16:02:07.341Z

【调用工具 Edit】```json
{"replace_all": false, "file_path": "C:/Users/18712/Downloads/伏光-面试题(4)/伏光-面试题/frontend/src/hooks/useWorkbench.ts", "old_string": "  const refreshVideoTasks = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const res = await listVideoTasks();\n      setVideoTasks(res.tasks);\n      setRunningCount(res.running_count);\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : \"视频任务列表加载失败，请稍后重试。\");\n    }\n  }, []);", "new_string": "  const refreshVideoTasks = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const res = await listVideoTasks();\n      // 合并而非替换：SSE 推送的实时状态是权威，全量刷新只填补本地缺失的任务，\n      // 不回退本地已由 video.progress / video.done 推进的状态（避免轮询竞态）。\n      setVideoTasks((prev) => {\n        const next = [...prev];\n        for (const st of res.tasks) {\n          if (!next.some((t) => t.id === st.id)) next.push(st);\n        }\n        return next;\n      });\n      setRunningCount(res.running_count);\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : \"视频任务列表加载失败，请稍后重试。\");\n    }\n  }, []);"}
```

## [Claude] 2026-11-18T16:08:35.978Z

## 交付总结（前端开发 Agent，Step 1–5 全部完成）

**边界遵守**：仅写入 `frontend/`；未读取 `docs/EVAL.md`、`evals/`、`backend/app/`、`backend/tests/`；未写入 `backend/`、`docs/`、`design/`、`evals/`。

**实现内容**（27 个源文件 + 6 个测试文件）：

- **Step 1 工程与令牌**：复用已有 Vite + React + TS + Tailwind 工程，`src/tokens.css` 与 `design/tokens.css` 逐字节一致（diff 验证）；56px 顶栏（标题 + 星标线性图标 + 唯一实心胶囊「+ 创建」）、左 260px / 中 max-width 720px / 右 300px 三栏骨架。
- **Step 2 左侧栏**：拖拽/点击上传（仅 .md/.docx/.doc，拒绝给完整句子提示）、文档列表（行高 40px、单行截断、注入隔离「已隔离」琥珀 tint 徽标、删除按钮）、底部历史问答列表（点击回看问题/回答/引用/视频状态）。
- **Step 3 问答主区**：搜索框式提问 + 快捷示例胶囊；SSE 客户端严格按 `docs/API_SPEC.md` §6 解析 9 类事件；答案上方横向 Sources 卡片，点击展开四维溯源（doc_id、source_file、heading_path、para_id + 原文段落 + 相似度）。
- **Step 4 视频工作室**：MP4 播放器（done 态）+ 队列面板，按 DESIGN.md §3 成对 tint 色块展示 queued（位次/前方人数）、running（实时进度条 + 阶段）、done（可播放链接）、failed（原因）；运行中计数 `n / 1`。修复了一处轮询竞态：全量刷新改为合并式，SSE 实时状态不被回退。
- **Step 5 测试**：Vitest + RTL 共 29 个测试，覆盖队列四态流转、引用卡片展开/收起、SSE 事件分发/容错/错误、历史切换与流式回答推进、上传扩展名拒绝。

1	/**
2	 * REST 客户端 —— 路径与字段严格对齐 docs/API_SPEC.md。
3	 * 仅做传输层，不做业务状态管理。
4	 */
5	import type { DocumentRecord, QaRecordDetail, QaRecordSummary, VideoTask } from "../types";
6	
7	const BASE = "/api";
8	
9	export class ApiError extends Error {
10	  code: string;
11	  detail: unknown;
12	  constructor(code: string, message: string, detail?: unknown) {
13	    super(message);
14	    this.code = code;
15	    this.detail = detail;
16	  }
17	}
18	
19	async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
20	  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, init);
21	  if (!res.ok) {
22	    let body: unknown = null;
23	    try {
24	      body = await res.json();
25	    } catch {
26	      /* 非 JSON 错误体 */
27	    }
28	    const err = (body as { error?: { code?: string; message?: string; detail?: unknown } })?.error;
29	    throw new ApiError(err?.code ?? `E_HTTP_${res.status}`, err?.message ?? res.statusText, err?.detail);
30	  }
31	  if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;
32	  return (await res.json()) as T;
33	}
34	
35	/* ---------- 文档（§2） ---------- */
36	
37	export async function uploadDocument(file: File): Promise<{ document: DocumentRecord }> {
38	  const form = new FormData();
39	  form.append("file", file);
40	  return request("/documents", { method: "POST", body: form });
41	}
42	
43	export async function listDocuments(): Promise<{ documents: DocumentRecord[]; total: number }> {
44	  return request("/documents");
45	}
46	
47	export async function deleteDocument(docId: string): Promise<void> {
48	  return request(`/documents/${encodeURIComponent(docId)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
49	}
50	
51	/* ---------- 问答（§3） ---------- */
52	
53	export async function listQaRecords(): Promise<{ records: QaRecordSummary[]; total: number }> {
54	  return request("/qa");
55	}
56	
57	export async function getQaRecord(id: number): Promise<{ record: QaRecordDetail }> {
58	  return request(`/qa/${id}`);
59	}

4	
5	/** RFC 3339 UTC → 「2024年4月21日」 */
6	export function formatDate(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
7	  if (!iso) return "";
8	  const d = new Date(iso);
9	  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "";
10	  return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}年${d.getUTCMonth() + 1}月${d.getUTCDate()}日`;
11	}
12	
13	/** RFC 3339 UTC → 「14:32」（UTC 时区，表格式对齐） */
14	export function formatTime(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
15	  if (!iso) return "";
16	  const d = new Date(iso);
17	  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "";
18	  const hh = String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, "0");
19	  const mm = String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, "0");
20	  return `${hh}:${mm}`;
21	}
22	
23	/** 0.0-1.0 → 「45%」 */
24	export function formatPercent(v: number): string {
25	  return `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`;

expect(screen.getByText(\"失败\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n\n  it(\"video_status 为 null 时不渲染状态徽标\", () => {\n    render(<HistoryList records={[rec({ video_status: null, video_task_id: null })]} \"./QuestionBox\";\n\ndescribe(\"QuestionBox 提问入口\", () => {\n  it(\"输入后点击「提问」提交并清空输入框\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const onAsk = vi.fn();\n    render(<QuestionBox onAsk={onAsk} disabled={false} examples={[]} />);\n    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(\"提问\"), \"研发投入是多少？\");\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"提问\" }));\n    expect(onAsk).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"研发投入是多少？\");\n    expect(screen.getByLabelText(\"提问\")).toHaveValue(\"\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"回车键同样提交\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const onAsk = vi.fn();\n    render(<QuestionBox onAsk={onAsk} disabled={false} examples={[]} />);\n    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(\"提问\"), \"问题A{enter}\");\n    expect(onAsk).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"问题A\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"空输入与纯空白不提交、按钮禁用\", () => {\n    const onAsk = vi.fn();\n    render(<QuestionBox onAsk={onAsk} disabled={false} examples={[]} />);\n    const btn = screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"提问\" });\n    expect(btn).toBeDisabled();\n    fireEvent.click(btn);\n    expect(onAsk).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"disabled 时输入框与示例按钮均禁用\", () => {\n    render(<QuestionBox onAsk={vi.fn()} disabled examples={[\"示例\"]} />);\n    expect(screen.getByLabelText(\"提问\")).toBeDisabled();\n    expect(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"示例\" })).toBeDisabled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"示例问题以胶囊按钮渲染并可直接提交\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const onAsk = vi.fn();\n    render(<QuestionBox onAsk={onAsk} disabled={false} examples={[\"众赢科技研发投入？\"]} />);\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"示例问题\")).toBeInTheDocument();\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"众赢科技研发投入？\" }));\n    expect(onAsk).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"众赢科技研发投入？\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"无示例时不渲染示例区\", () => {\n    render(<QuestionBox onAsk={vi.fn()} disabled={false} examples={[]} />);\n    expect(screen.queryByText(\"示例问题\")).not.toBeInTheDocument();\n  });\n});\n"}

```

\"GET\";\n      if (method === \"DELETE\" && url === \"/api/documents/doc02\" && failDelete)\n        return new Response(null, { status: 500, statusText: \"boom\" });\n      if (method === \"DELETE\") return new Response(null, { status: 204 });\n      if (url === \"/api/documents\") return Response.json({ documents: [], total: 0 });\n      if (url === \"/api/qa\") return Response.json({ records: [], total: 0 });\n      if (url === \"/api/video-tasks\") return Response.json({ tasks: [], running_count: 0 });\n      return new Response(null, { status: 404 });\n    });\n    vi.stubGlobal(\"fetch\", fetchMock);\n    const { result } = renderHook(() => useWorkbench());\n    await act(() => result.current.removeDocument(\"doc01\"));\n    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"/api/documents/doc01\", expect.objectContaining({ method: \"DELETE\" }));\n    expect(result.current.error).toBeNull();\n\n    failDelete = true;\n    await act(() => result.current.removeDocument(\"doc02\"));\n    expect(result.current.error).toMatch(/E_HTTP_500|删除失败/);\n  });"}
